00021: def beam(parent,name,a,b,width,depth,color):
00022:     dx,dy,dz=(b[i]-a[i] for i in range(3))
00023:     length=math.sqrt(dx*dx+dy*dy+dz*dz)
00024:     q=box((width,depth,length),center=tuple((a[i]+b[i])/2 for i in range(3)),color=color)
00025:     q.turn('y',math.degrees(math.acos(dz/length)))
00026:     q.turn('z',math.degrees(math.atan2(dy,dx)))
00027:     return add(parent,name,q)

00018:     parent.include(name,node)

00021: def beam(parent,name,a,b,width,depth,color):
00022:     dx,dy,dz=(b[i]-a[i] for i in range(3))
00023:     length=math.sqrt(dx*dx+dy*dy+dz*dz)
00024:     q=box((width,depth,length),center=tuple((a[i]+b[i])/2 for i in range(3)),color=color)
00025:     q.turn('y',math.degrees(math.acos(dz/length)))
00026:     q.turn('z',math.degrees(math.atan2(dy,dx)))
00027:     return add(parent,name,q)

00174: realize(scene)
00175: # Procedural weave and tiny faded pink motifs, without image textures.
00176: for obj in bpy.context.scene.objects:
00177:     if obj.type!='MESH' or not obj.data.materials:
00178:         continue
00179:     mat=obj.data.materials[0]
00180:     mat.use_nodes=True
00181:     bs=mat.node_tree.nodes.get('Principled BSDF')
00182:     if bs:
00183:         bs.inputs['Roughness'].default_value=.76
00184:     if 'upholstery' in obj.name.lower() or 'seat' in obj.name.lower() or 'wing' in obj.name.lower() or 'back' in obj.name.lower():
00185:         nodes=mat.node_tree.nodes
00186:         links=mat.node_tree.links
00187:         tex=nodes.new('ShaderNodeTexNoise')
00188:         tex.inputs['Scale'].default_value=180
00189:         tex.inputs['Detail'].default_value=2
00190:         bump=nodes.new('ShaderNodeBump')
00191:         bump.inputs['Strength'].default_value=.13
00192:         bump.inputs['Distance'].default_value=.012
00193:         links.new(tex.outputs['Fac'],bump.inputs['Height'])
00194:         links.new(bump.outputs['Normal'],bs.inputs['Normal'])
00195:         if 'pillow' not in obj.name.lower():
00196:             coord=nodes.new('ShaderNodeTexCoord')
00197:             mapping=nodes.new('ShaderNodeVectorMath')
00198:             mapping.operation='MULTIPLY'
00199:             mapping.inputs[1].default_value=(1.1,4.4,1.1)
00200:             links.new(coord.outputs['Generated'],mapping.inputs[0])
00201:             vor=nodes.new('ShaderNodeTexVoronoi')
00202:             vor.inputs['Scale'].default_value=24
00203:             links.new(mapping.outputs['Vector'],vor.inputs['Vector'])
00204:             ramp=nodes.new('ShaderNodeValToRGB')
00205:             ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].position=.055
00206:             ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].color=(.48,.25,.23,1)
00207:             ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].position=.115
00208:             ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].color=(*fabric,1)
00209:             links.new(vor.outputs['Distance'],ramp.inputs[0])
00210:             links.new(ramp.outputs['Color'],bs.inputs['Base Color'])
00211: 
00212: set_view(CAMERA,TARGET,lens=LENS,sensor=36)
00213: sc=bpy.context.scene
00214: if sc.world is None:
00215:     sc.world=bpy.data.worlds.new('Ambient')
00216: sc.world.use_nodes=True
00217: sc.world.node_tree.nodes['Background'].inputs['Color'].default_value=(.72,.75,.79,1)
00218: sc.world.node_tree.nodes['Background'].inputs['Strength'].default_value=.32
00219: area_emitter((-3.4,-.1,3.0),power=450,size=2.5,target=(-.2,0,.6),color=(1,.94,.83))
